# Create project structure and files for Mobii3usWhisperd-0.3
import os, json, textwrap, zipfile, pathlib, io

root = "/mnt/data/Mobii3usWhisperd-0.3"
server_dir = os.path.join(root, "server")
web_dir = os.path.join(root, "web")
os.makedirs(server_dir, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(web_dir, exist_ok=True)

# server/app.py with full features (SSE, modes, prefilter, mock loop)
app_py = r'''
# Whisper++ Rebuild – Tri-Lane Realtime Engine (Flask SSE MVP)
# Version: 0.3 – Surface programmable modes + deterministic prefilter
import os, json, time, queue, threading, datetime as dt, re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from flask import Flask, Response, request, jsonify, send_from_directory
from flask_cors import CORS

app = Flask(__name__)
CORS(app, resources={r"/*": {"origins": "*"}})

# --- In-memory event bus per stream_id ---
SUBS: Dict[str, "queue.Queue[dict]"] = {}
STREAM_MODE: Dict[str, str] = {}  # stream_id -> surface mode

def publish(stream_id: str, payload: dict):
    SUBS.setdefault(stream_id, queue.Queue(maxsize=1024)).put(payload)

def sse(stream_id: str):
    q = SUBS.setdefault(stream_id, queue.Queue(maxsize=1024))
    while True:
        item = q.get()
        yield f"id: {item['segment_id']}\n"
        yield "event: segment\n"
        yield "data: " + json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n\n"

@app.get("/events/paired")
def events_paired():
    stream_id = request.args.get("stream_id", "default")
    return Response(sse(stream_id), mimetype="text/event-stream")

# ---------- Mock Whisper++ loop (replace with your decoder) ----------
@dataclass
class Token:
    t_ms: int
    txt: str
    id: int

@dataclass
class Segment:
    stream_id: str
    segment_id: int
    start_ms: int
    end_ms: int
    text: str
    tokens: List[Token]

# Underlayer capture — raw text (hook your actual capture here)
def fractal_capture(text: str) -> str:
    return text

# ---- Surface programmable modes ----
SURFACE_MODES = ("filtered", "reframed", "denerdify", "to3yo")

PROFANITY = [r"\bshit\b", r"\bfuck(?:ing|er)?\b", r"\basshole\b", r"\bdamn\b"]
def profanity_mask(text: str) -> str:
    def mask(m):
        w = m.group(0)
        return w[0] + "—"*(max(1, len(w)-1))
    out = text
    for pat in PROFANITY:
        out = re.sub(pat, mask, out, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    return out

REFRAME_MAP = {
  r"\bproblem(s)?\b": r"challenge\1",
  r"\bcan[’']?t\b": "can—with a tweak",
  r"\bfail(?:ure|ed)?\b": "learning step",
  r"\bissue(s)?\b": r"item\1 to improve"
}
def reframe_silver_lining(text: str) -> str:
    out = text
    for pat, rep in REFRAME_MAP.items():
        out = re.sub(pat, rep, out, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    if re.search(r"[.!?]\s*$", out):
        out = re.sub(r"([.!?])\s*$", r" — next step noted\1", out)
    return out

JARGON = {
  r"\bgeotechnical\b": "ground testing",
  r"\bsubsurface\b": "underground",
  r"\bhydrogeology\b": "water in the ground",
  r"\blidar\b": "laser mapping",
  r"\borthomosaic\b": "stitched map photo",
  r"\bCPT\b": "cone test (CPT)",
  r"\bSPT\b": "hammer blow test (SPT)"
}
def denerdify_terms(text: str) -> str:
    out = text
    for pat, rep in JARGON.items():
        out = re.sub(pat, rep, out, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    return out

SAFETY_KEEP = re.compile(r"\b(alert|danger|stop|poison|electric|gas|leak|fire|flood|shock)\b", re.IGNORECASE)
def to_three_year_old(text: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"[,;:]", ".", text)
    parts = [p.strip() for p in re.split(r"[.!?]", s) if p.strip()]
    simple = []
    for p in parts[:3]:
        if SAFETY_KEEP.search(p):
            simple.append(p)
        else:
            words = p.split()
            short = " ".join(words[:8])
            simple.append(short)
    return ". ".join(simple) + "." if simple else ""

def apply_surface_mode(text: str, mode: str) -> str:
    mode = (mode or "filtered").lower()
    if mode not in SURFACE_MODES:
        mode = "filtered"
    if mode == "filtered":
        return profanity_mask(text)
    if mode == "reframed":
        return reframe_silver_lining(profanity_mask(text))
    if mode == "denerdify":
        return denerdify_terms(profanity_mask(text))
    if mode == "to3yo":
        return to_three_year_old(profanity_mask(text))
    return text

# ---- Deterministic prefilter (collapse → shorten → length gates) ----
SAFE_UNITS = r"(?:cm|mm|m|km|ft|yd|mi|°C|°F|psi|kPa|Pa|mph|m\/s|g|kg|lb|L|ml|mL|Hz|kHz|MHz|GHz)"
FILLERS = r"(?:um+|uh+|erm+|like|you know|i mean|kinda|sorta|basically|literally|actually)"
FILLER_RE = re.compile(rf"(?:\b{FILLERS}\b[,.\-–— ]*)+", re.I)
CHAR_STUTTER = re.compile(r"\b([a-z])(?:-\1){1,}([a-z]+)\b", re.I)
LONG_VOWEL   = re.compile(r"([aeiouAEIOU])\1{2,}")
DUP_WORDS = re.compile(r"\b(\w+)(\s+\1\b)+", re.I)
SHORT_MAP = {
  "classification":"class",
  "geotechnical":"geotech",
  "subsurface":"underground",
  "hydrogeology":"water-in-ground",
  "approximately":"~",
  "utilization":"use",
  "utilize":"use",
  "measurement":"measure",
  "coordinate":"coord",
}

def repetition_collapse(text: str) -> str:
    t = CHAR_STUTTER.sub(lambda m: m.group(1)+m.group(2), text)
    t = LONG_VOWEL.sub(r"\1\1", t)
    t = DUP_WORDS.sub(r"\1", t)
    t = FILLER_RE.sub("", t)
    t = re.sub(r"\s{2,}", " ", t).strip()
    return t

def lexical_shorten(text: str) -> str:
    def repl(m):
        w = m.group(0); k = w.lower()
        if k in SHORT_MAP:
            rep = SHORT_MAP[k]
            return rep if w.islower() else rep.capitalize()
        return w
    return re.sub(r"[A-Za-z]+", repl, text)

SAFETY = SAFETY_KEEP
def keep_tokens(token: str) -> bool:
    if re.fullmatch(r"\d+(\.\d+)?", token): return True
    if re.fullmatch(SAFE_UNITS, token):     return True
    if SAFETY.search(token):                 return True
    return False

def length_gate(text: str, max_word_len: int, max_words: int) -> str:
    parts = re.split(r"([.!?])", text)
    out = []
    for i in range(0, len(parts), 2):
        sent = (parts[i] or "").strip()
        if not sent: continue
        words = sent.split()
        new_words = []
        for w in words:
            if keep_tokens(w):
                new_words.append(w)
            else:
                core = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z]", "", w)
                if len(core) > max_word_len:
                    core = core[:max_word_len]
                    w = core
                new_words.append(w)
            if len(new_words) >= max_words: break
        out.append(" ".join(new_words))
    return ". ".join(out).strip() + ("." if out else "")

def surface_prefilter(text: str, mode: str) -> str:
    t = repetition_collapse(text)
    t = lexical_shorten(t)
    mode = (mode or "filtered").lower()
    if   mode == "filtered":
        return length_gate(t, max_word_len=12, max_words=25)
    elif mode == "reframed":
        return length_gate(t, max_word_len=10, max_words=20)
    elif mode == "denerdify":
        return length_gate(t, max_word_len=8,  max_words=15)
    elif mode == "to3yo":
        return length_gate(t, max_word_len=5,  max_words=5)
    return t

def overlay_from(seg: Segment) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    t = list(range(seg.start_ms, seg.end_ms, 30))
    v = [0.15 + 0.05*((i//2)%3) for i,_ in enumerate(t)]
    return {"spectral": None, "fractal": [{"t_ms": t[i], "v": v[i]} for i in range(len(t))], "markers":[]}

def token_grid_from(seg: Segment) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    return [{"t_ms": tk.t_ms, "txt": tk.txt, "id": tk.id, "emb": [0.0, 0.1], "flags": []} for tk in seg.tokens]

def publish_segment(seg: Segment):
    mode = STREAM_MODE.get(seg.stream_id, "filtered")
    base = surface_prefilter(seg.text, mode)
    styled = apply_surface_mode(base, mode)
    payload = {
        "stream_id": seg.stream_id,
        "segment_id": seg.segment_id,
        "start_ms": seg.start_ms,
        "end_ms": seg.end_ms,
        "mode": mode,
        "surface": styled,
        "underlayer": fractal_capture(seg.text),
        "overlay": overlay_from(seg),
        "token_grid": token_grid_from(seg)
    }
    publish(seg.stream_id, payload)

def mock_decoder_loop(stream_id="default"):
    base = int(time.time()*1000)
    samples = [
        "we… we… uh we absolutely must classify this geotechnical subsurface profile at 12.7 kPa, it's kind of a problem",
        "testing the fractal overlay and token grid with lidar orthomosaic and hydrogeology terms",
        "and we keep it safe if there is electric or gas leak danger",
    ]
    for seg_id, txt in enumerate(samples):
        start = base + seg_id*1400
        toks = []
        tcur = start
        for i, w in enumerate(txt.split(" ")):
            toks.append(Token(t_ms=tcur, txt=(" " if i else "")+w, id=1000+i))
            tcur += 120 + 12*len(w)
        seg = Segment(stream_id, seg_id, start, tcur, txt, toks)
        publish_segment(seg)
        time.sleep(0.4)

@app.post("/ingest/start")
def start_stream():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    stream_id = data.get("stream_id", "default")
    if "mode" in data:
        STREAM_MODE[stream_id] = data["mode"]
    threading.Thread(target=mock_decoder_loop, args=(stream_id,), daemon=True).start()
    return {"ok": True, "stream_id": stream_id, "mode": STREAM_MODE.get(stream_id, "filtered"), "ts": dt.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}

@app.post("/surface/mode")
def set_mode():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    stream_id = data.get("stream_id","default")
    mode = data.get("mode","filtered")
    STREAM_MODE[stream_id] = mode
    return {"ok": True, "stream_id": stream_id, "mode": mode}

@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"ok": True}

# Static hosting for the demo page
@app.route("/web/<path:fname>")
def webfile(fname):
    base = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "web")
    return send_from_directory(base, fname)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run("0.0.0.0", 5001, debug=True)
'''

open(os.path.join(server_dir, "app.py"), "w").write(app_py)

  ["denerdify","De-Nerdify"],

  toggleRawBtn.textContent = showRaw ? 'Surface view' : 'Show Underlayer';

let currentMode = "filtered";
function renderModeButtons(){

        method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
        body: JSON.stringify({stream_id: streamId, mode: val})
      });
    };
    modesDiv.appendChild(b);
  });
}
renderModeButtons();

const es = new EventSource(`/events/paired?stream_id=${encodeURIComponent(streamId)}`);

    tokensDiv.appendChild(d);

});

// kick off demo stream if requested via hash

  fetch('/ingest/start', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({stream_id: streamId, mode: currentMode})});

# README.md
readme = r'''
# Möbii³us Whisperd – Tri-Lane Realtime Engine (v0.3)

A deterministic, time-locked speech-to-text presentation stack:

- **Surface**: live text with programmable logic (Filtered, Reframed, De-Nerdify, To-a-3-yo) + waveform/spectrogram + fractal overlays
- **Underlayer**: raw transcript (unaltered) for audit/analysis
- **Token/Grid**: blocky token analytics, embeddings, flags — synced to the same clock

## Run (dev)

```bash
python -m venv .venv && source .venv/bin/activate
pip install flask flask-cors
python server/app.py

Open the demo page: http://localhost:5001/web/WhisperTriLane.html#demo
or the branded site page: http://localhost:5001/web/doinbetter_whisperd.html
API

    GET /events/paired?stream_id=default — SSE stream; each event includes {surface, underlayer, overlay, token_grid, start_ms, end_ms}

    POST /ingest/start — start mock stream: {"stream_id":"demo","mode":"filtered"}

    POST /surface/mode — set surface transform mode: {"stream_id":"demo","mode":"denerdify"}

See server/openapi.yaml for details.
Swap in your real Whisper++

Replace mock_decoder_loop() with your decoder on_segment callback. Build a Segment with token times, call publish_segment(seg).
Notes

    Underlayer is never changed.

    Surface applies: Prefilter (collapse/disfluency/shorten/length gates) → Mode (Filtered/Reframed/De-Nerdify/To-3-yo).

    Numbers, units and safety terms are preserved by design.

    SSE by default; migrate to WebSockets for bi-directional edits when ready.

    The web/doinbetter_whisperd.html page is upload-ready to your site and embeds the tri-lane UI via iframe.

© DoinBetter — Möbii³us
'''
open(os.path.join(root, "README.md"), "w").write(readme)

         Add all that to "Buddy, let’s rebuild Whisper++ as a **stacked, time-locked tri-lane** engine: **(A) Surface:** live text (clean) + waveform/spectrogram + fractal overlays **(B) Underlayer:** raw text (unaltered) held in parallel **(C) Token/Grid:** blocky token analytics, embeddings, flags — synced to the same clock   Below is a lean, production-ready scaffold you can paste into your repo and iterate. It’s split into: architecture, data contracts, backend (Flask) real-time pipeline, and a React UI that renders the three lanes in lock-step.   ---   # 1) Architecture (from mic → UI)   

mermaid flowchart LR   Mic[(Mic / Stream)] --> A[Capture/Resample 16k]   A --> B[Whisper++ Decoder]   B -->|segments w/ token times| C{Analysis Fanout}   C --> D[Fractal Overlay Engine] --> K[Overlay frames]   C --> E[Surface Filter] --> L[Surface text]   C --> F[Raw Capture] --> M[Underlayer text]   C --> G[Token Analytics] --> N[Token grid/embeddings/flags]   K --> O[SSE/WebSocket Multiplexer]   L --> O   M --> O   N --> O   O --> P[React UI: Surface Lane]   O --> Q[React UI: Underlayer Lane]   O --> R[React UI: Token/Grid Lane]

   * **Single Source of Truth:** every segment gets {segment_id, start_ms, end_ms, tokens[]}. All products (surface/raw/overlay/grid) reference that segment\_id & times. * **Clock discipline:** use Whisper timestamps as authoritative media time; use time.monotonic_ns() only for transport/latency stats.   ---   # 2) Data contracts (JSON)   

json // /events/paired stream payload {   "stream_id": "sess_a12f7c",   "segment_id": 128,   "start_ms": 38120,   "end_ms": 41670,   "surface": "Cleaned text…",   "underlayer": "Raw f****** text…",   "overlay": {     "spectral": "data-url-or-chunk-id",     "fractal": [       {"t_ms": 38120, "v": 0.18}, {"t_ms": 38150, "v": 0.22}     ],     "markers": [{"t_ms": 39000, "type":"emph"}]   },   "token_grid": [     {"t_ms":38120,"txt":"We","id":901,"emb":[0.12,-0.04,...],"flags":["stop"]},     {"t_ms":38380,"txt":" are","id":456,"emb":[...],"flags":[]}   ] }

   * **overlay.spectral**: either a thin PNG data-URL of the current slice or a small numeric array; **fractal** is your time-series. * **token\_grid.emb**: keep short (e.g., PCA-reduced), or serve on a side endpoint.   ---   # 3) Backend — Flask real-time engine (drop-in MVP)   server/app.py (core endpoints + SSE + placeholders you can wire to your actual Whisper build)   

python # Whisper++ Rebuild – Tri-Lane Realtime Engine (Flask SSE MVP) # server/app.py import os, json, time, queue, threading, datetime as dt from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict from typing import List, Dict, Any from flask import Flask, Response, request, jsonify from flask_cors import CORS   app = Flask(__name__) CORS(app, resources={r"/*": {"origins": "*"}})   # --- In-memory event bus per stream_id --- SUBS: Dict[str, "queue.Queue[dict]"] = {}   def publish(stream_id: str, payload: dict):     SUBS.setdefault(stream_id, queue.Queue(maxsize=1024)).put(payload)   def sse(stream_id: str):     q = SUBS.setdefault(stream_id, queue.Queue(maxsize=1024))     while True:         item = q.get()         yield f"id: {item['segment_id']}\n"         yield "event: segment\n"         yield "data: " + json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n\n"   @app.get("/events/paired") def events_paired():     stream_id = request.args.get("stream_id", "default")     return Response(sse(stream_id), mimetype="text/event-stream")   # ---------- Mock Whisper++ loop (replace with your decoder) ---------- @dataclass class Token:     t_ms: int     txt: str     id: int   @dataclass class Segment:     stream_id: str     segment_id: int     start_ms: int     end_ms: int     text: str     tokens: List[Token]   def fractal_capture(text: str) -> str:     # raw underlayer (hook your engine here)     return text   def fractal_filter(text: str) -> str:     # clean/surface (mask policy here)     return text.replace("fuck", "f—").replace("shit", "s—")   def overlay_from(seg: Segment) -> Dict[str, Any]:     # Simulate a fractal trace and a small spectral slice     t = list(range(seg.start_ms, seg.end_ms, 30))     v = [0.15 + 0.05*((i//2)%3) for i,_ in enumerate(t)]     return {"spectral": None, "fractal": [{"t_ms": t[i], "v": v[i]} for i in range(len(t))], "markers":[]}   def token_grid_from(seg: Segment) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:     return [{"t_ms": tk.t_ms, "txt": tk.txt, "id": tk.id, "emb": [0.0, 0.1], "flags": []} for tk in seg.tokens]   def publish_segment(seg: Segment):     payload = {         "stream_id": seg.stream_id,         "segment_id": seg.segment_id,         "start_ms": seg.start_ms,         "end_ms": seg.end_ms,         "surface": fractal_filter(seg.text),         "underlayer": fractal_capture(seg.text),         "overlay": overlay_from(seg),         "token_grid": token_grid_from(seg)     }     publish(seg.stream_id, payload)   def mock_decoder_loop(stream_id="default"):     # Emits a few segments with token times; replace with Whisper callbacks     base = int(time.time()*1000)     for seg_id, txt in enumerate(["We are live", "testing the fractal overlay", "and token grid"]):         start = base + seg_id*1200         toks = []         tcur = start         for i, w in enumerate(txt.split(" ")):             toks.append(Token(t_ms=tcur, txt=(" " if i else "")+w, id=1000+i))             tcur += 120 + 12*len(w)         seg = Segment(stream_id, seg_id, start, tcur, txt, toks)         publish_segment(seg)         time.sleep(0.35)   @app.post("/ingest/start") def start_stream():     stream_id = request.json.get("stream_id", "default")     threading.Thread(target=mock_decoder_loop, args=(stream_id,), daemon=True).start()     return {"ok": True, "stream_id": stream_id, "ts": dt.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}   @app.get("/health") def health():     return {"ok": True}   if __name__ == "__main__":     app.run("0.0.0.0", 5001, debug=True)

   **Swap the mock loop with your real Whisper++ bindings:**   * Register the **on\_segment** callback. * Build Segment(stream_id, segment_id, start_ms, end_ms, text, tokens) from Whisper token times. * Call publish_segment(seg) to fan-out surface/underlayer/overlay/grid in one atomic packet.   ---   # 4) Front-end — React tri-lane UI (stacked, time-synced)   src/components/WhisperTriLane.tsx   

tsx import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";   function useSSE(url: string) {   const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);   const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);   useEffect(() => {     const es = new EventSource(url);     es.onopen = () => setConnected(true);     es.addEventListener("segment", (ev: MessageEvent) => {       const seg = JSON.parse(ev.data);       setItems(prev => (prev.length && prev.at(-1).segment_id === seg.segment_id) ? prev : [...prev, seg]);     });     es.onerror = () => setConnected(false);     return () => es.close();   }, [url]);   return { items, connected }; }   function FractalStrip({ overlay }:{overlay:any}) {   // Tiny sparkline from overlay.fractal   return (     <div className="h-8 w-full bg-black/80 rounded">       <svg width="100%" height="100%" preserveAspectRatio="none">         {overlay?.fractal?.length ? (() => {           const pts = overlay.fractal;           const xs = pts.map((_,i)=> i/(pts.length-1));           const ys = pts.map(p=> 1 - Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (p.v-0)/(0.5))));           const d = xs.map((x,i)=>`${i?'L':'M'} ${x*100} ${ys[i]*100}`).join(" ");           return <path d={d} stroke="white" fill="none" strokeWidth="1"/>         })() : null}       </svg>     </div>   ); }   export default function WhisperTriLane({ streamId="default" }:{streamId?:string}) {   const { items, connected } = useSSE(`/events/paired?stream_id=${streamId}`);   const [viewRaw, setViewRaw] = useState(false);     return (     <div className="grid gap-3">       <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">         <span className={connected ? "text-green-500" : "text-red-500"}>●</span>         <button className="px-2 py-1 border rounded" onClick={()=>setViewRaw(v=>!v)}>           {viewRaw ? "Surface view" : "Show Underlayer"}         </button>       </div>         {/* SURFACE lane (text + fractal overlay strip) */}       <div className="grid gap-2">         <FractalStrip overlay={items.at(-1)?.overlay}/>         <div className="rounded border p-2 bg-white/80">           {items.map(s => (             <p key={s.segment_id} data-t0={s.start_ms} data-t1={s.end_ms}>               {s.surface}             </p>           ))}         </div>       </div>         {/* UNDERLAYER lane (toggle) */}       {viewRaw && (         <div className="rounded border p-2 bg-amber-50/80">           {items.map(s => (             <p key={s.segment_id} className="opacity-80">               {s.underlayer}             </p>           ))}         </div>       )}         {/* TOKEN GRID lane (block view) */}       <div className="rounded border p-2 bg-slate-50">         <div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-1 text-xs">           {items.at(-1)?.token_grid?.map((t:any, i:number) => (             <div key={i} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 bg-white">               <div className="truncate">{t.txt}</div>               <div className="opacity-60">t={t.t_ms}</div>             </div>           ))}         </div>       </div>     </div>   ); }

   **Layout guidance (per your spec):**   * Place this component **below** the conversation workspace editor, above the user input bar. * The fractal strip sits **above** the surface text block; the token grid sits **below** as the blocky lane. * Underlayer/Raw is role-gated or toggled.   ---   # 5) Time sync & stability (baked in)   * **Authoritative media time:** start_ms/end_ms from Whisper tokens. * **Atomic delivery:** surface/underlayer/overlay/grid shipped in one event → no jitter between lanes. * **Jitter buffer:** UI simply appends; (optional) keep last N segments if you want a rolling window. * **Edits later:** If you support post-hoc corrections, emit event: revise with the same segment_id.   ---   # 6) Start/Smoke test

import zipfile
import os
from pathlib import Path

# Paths to earlier uploaded ZIPs that were not merged yet
zips_to_merge = [
    "/mnt/data/DoinBetter_FractalFieldTheory_Repo (1).zip",
    "/mnt/data/Mobius_Social_Promo_Kit (1).zip",
    "/mnt/data/Mobius_DoinBetter_Ecosystem_AllRepos_FINALFINAL(1) (1).zip",
    "/mnt/data/assets.zip",
    "/mnt/data/Mobiius_DoinBetter_Full_Project_Bundle.zip"  # previously created main bundle
]

# Output master package path
master_zip_path = "/mnt/data/Mobiius_DoinBetter_Master_Ark.zip"

# Helper: extract all zips into a temp dir
temp_dir = Path("/mnt/data/temp_master_bundle")
if temp_dir.exists():
    import shutil
    shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)
temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True)

for zip_path in zips_to_merge:
    if os.path.exists(zip_path):
        try:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'r') as zf:
                zf.extractall(temp_dir)
        except zipfile.BadZipFile:
            # Skip files that are not zips
            pass

# Now compress everything from temp_dir into the master ark
with zipfile.ZipFile(master_zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as master_zip:
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(temp_dir):
        for file in files:
            file_path = Path(root) / file
            arcname = file_path.relative_to(temp_dir)
            master_zip.write(file_path, arcname)

# Mobiiius Scripts Pack — Deploy & Admin

This pack contains production-ready helper scripts to deploy, publish, and sanity-check the Mobiiius site.

## What\'s included
- deploy/drive_upload.py — Google Drive upload via OAuth (stores token.json)
- deploy/ftp_deploy.py — cPanel/Namecheap-friendly FTPS uploader to public_html
- deploy/github_publish.sh — Create/push a GitHub repo from local bundles
- deploy/docker-compose.yml — Nginx (SPA) + Gunicorn/Flask API
- deploy/Dockerfile.api — Minimal Python image for the API
- deploy/nginx.conf — Static SPA + /api reverse proxy + cache headers
- scripts/brand_rename.sh — Standardize brand spelling to “Mobiiius”
- scripts/build_release.py — Zip + checksums + RELEASE_INDEX.json
- scripts/seed_defaults.py — Create .env defaults (idempotent)
- scripts/dev.sh — Tiny local dev runner
- scripts/test_email.py — STARTTLS SMTP smoke test
- scripts/test_usgs.py — Pull USGS IV (00060 discharge) by bbox

**Safety:** Scripts prefer env vars and avoid storing secrets in plaintext. Review before use.
'

# deploy/drive_upload.py  (Google Drive upload)
w "$ROOT/deploy/drive_upload.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import os, sys, json
from typing import Optional
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials
from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import InstalledAppFlow
from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from googleapiclient.http import MediaFileUpload
from google.auth.transport.requests import Request

SCOPES = ["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/drive.file"]

def ensure_creds() -> Credentials:
    creds: Optional[Credentials] = None
    if os.path.exists("token.json"):
        creds = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file("token.json", SCOPES)
    if not creds or not creds.valid:
        if creds and creds.expired and creds.refresh_token:
            creds.refresh(Request())
        else:
            if not os.path.exists("credentials.json"):
                raise SystemExit("Place your OAuth client as credentials.json next to this script.")
            flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file("credentials.json", SCOPES)
            creds = flow.run_local_server(port=0)
        with open("token.json", "w") as f:
            f.write(creds.to_json())
    return creds

def upload(path: str, parent_id: Optional[str] = None):
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        raise SystemExit(f"Not a file: {path}")
    service = build("drive", "v3", credentials=ensure_creds())
    name = os.path.basename(path)
    media = MediaFileUpload(path, resumable=True)
    body = {"name": name}
    if parent_id: body["parents"] = [parent_id]
    file = service.files().create(body=body, media_body=media, fields="id,name,webViewLink,webContentLink").execute()
    print(json.dumps(file, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python deploy/drive_upload.py <file> [parent_folder_id]")
        sys.exit(1)
    upload(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv)>2 else None)
'

# deploy/ftp_deploy.py (FTPS for cPanel/Namecheap)
w "$ROOT/deploy/ftp_deploy.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os, sys, ftplib, pathlib
host = os.getenv("FTP_HOST")
user = os.getenv("FTP_USER")
pw   = os.getenv("FTP_PASS")
remote = os.getenv("FTP_REMOTE_DIR","public_html")
local = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else "./Mobiiius_Frontend_SPA"
if not all([host,user,pw]): raise SystemExit("Set FTP_HOST, FTP_USER, FTP_PASS")
ftps = ftplib.FTP_TLS(host); ftps.login(user,pw); ftps.prot_p()
def upload_dir(local_dir, remote_dir):
    local_dir = os.path.abspath(local_dir)
    try: ftps.mkd(remote_dir)
    except Exception: pass
    ftps.cwd(remote_dir)
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(local_dir):
        rel = os.path.relpath(root, local_dir)
        # enter nested dirs if any
        if rel != ".":
            for p in pathlib.Path(rel).parts:
                try: ftps.mkd(p)
                except Exception: pass
                ftps.cwd(p)
        for f in files:
            with open(os.path.join(root,f),"rb") as fh:
                ftps.storbinary(f"STOR "+f, fh)
        if rel != ".":
            ftps.cwd("/".join([".."]*len(pathlib.Path(rel).parts)))
upload_dir(local, remote); ftps.quit()
'

# deploy/github_publish.sh
w "$ROOT/deploy/github_publish.sh" '
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
REPO_DIR=${1:-Mobiiius_Repo}
REMOTE_NAME=${2:-origin}
REMOTE_URL=${3:-}
mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR"
rsync -a --delete Mobiiius_Frontend_SPA/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_API_Server/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Docs/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Benchmarks/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Deployment/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Extensions/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
cd "$REPO_DIR"
git init -q
git add -A
git commit -m "Mobiiius: aggregate build" >/dev/null
git branch -M main || true
if [[ -n "$REMOTE_URL" ]]; then
  git remote add "$REMOTE_NAME" "$REMOTE_URL" 2>/dev/null || true
  git push -u "$REMOTE_NAME" main || git push -u "$REMOTE_NAME" master
else
  echo "Add a remote to push: git remote add origin <URL> && git push -u origin main"
fi
'

  location ~* \.(?:js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|svg|ico)$ {
    expires 7d; add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=604800";
  }
  location /api/ {
    proxy_pass http://api:5000/;
    proxy_http_version 1.1;
    proxy_set_header Host $host;

    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
  }
}
'

# scripts/brand_rename.sh
w "$ROOT/scripts/brand_rename.sh" '
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
rg -n --hidden --no-ignore -S "(Möbii?u?s|Mobii?u?s|Möbii³us|Mobii³us|Möbius|Mobius)" || true
read -p "Proceed with replacements to '\''Mobiiius'\''? [y/N] " ans
if [[ "${ans:-N}" != "y" && "${ans:-N}" != "Y" ]]; then exit 0; fi
variants=("Möbii³us" "Möbiius" "Möbius" "Mobii³us" "Mobiius" "Mobius")
for v in "${variants[@]}"; do
  files=$(rg -l "$v" || true)
  if [[ -n "$files" ]]; then sed -i.bak "s/$v/Mobiiius/g" $files || true; fi
done
echo "Done. Review diffs. Remove backups with: find . -name '\''*.bak'\'' -delete"
'

# scripts/build_release.py
w "$ROOT/scripts/build_release.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os, zipfile, hashlib, json, datetime, argparse
def sha256(p):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p,"rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(8192), b""): h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()
def zipdir(src, zipf, base=""):
    for root,_,files in os.walk(src):
        for f in files:
            fp=os.path.join(root,f); rel=os.path.join(base, os.path.relpath(fp,src)); zipf.write(fp,rel)
ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--source", default=".", help="Folder to package"); ap.add_argument("--out", default="Mobiiius_Bundle.zip"); a=ap.parse_args()
with zipfile.ZipFile(a.out,"w",zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z: zipdir(a.source, z)
idx={"generated_utc":datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()+"Z","zip":{"path":a.out,"bytes":os.path.getsize(a.out),"sha256":sha256(a.out)}}
open("RELEASE_INDEX.json","w").write(json.dumps(idx,indent=2)); print(json.dumps(idx,indent=2))
'

if not os.path.exists(ENV_PATH):
    with open(ENV_PATH,"w") as f:
        for k,v in defaults.items(): f.write(f"{k}={v}\n")

# scripts/test_email.py
w "$ROOT/scripts/test_email.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os, smtplib, ssl
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
host=os.getenv("SMTP_HOST","localhost"); port=int(os.getenv("SMTP_PORT","587"))
user=os.getenv("SMTP_USER",""); pw=os.getenv("SMTP_PASS","")
sender=os.getenv("SMTP_FROM","Mobiiius <noreply@example.com>"); rcpt=os.getenv("SMTP_TO","owner@example.com")
msg=MIMEText("Mobiiius SMTP test ok."); msg["Subject"]="Mobiiius SMTP test"; msg["From"]=sender; msg["To"]=rcpt
ctx=ssl.create_default_context()
with smtplib.SMTP(host,port) as s:
    s.starttls(context=ctx)
    if user and pw: s.login(user,pw)
    s.sendmail(sender,[rcpt],msg.as_string())
print("Sent test email to", rcpt)
'

#!/usr/bin/env python3

params={"format":"json","bBox":bbox,"parameterCd":os.getenv("USGS_PARAM","00060"),"siteType":"ST"}
url="https://waterservices.usgs.gov/nwis/iv/?"+urllib.parse.urlencode(params)
print("GET",url)
with urllib.request.urlopen(url) as r: data=json.loads(r.read().decode("utf-8"))
print(json.dumps({"series":len(data.get("value",{}).get("timeSeries",[]))}, indent=2))
'

 Invite Link: https://www.mobileapp.app/to/sszavOR?ref=m_clOne-paragraph picture (elevator)Mobiiius fuses messy, multi-modal data into one explained answer per tick, then moves it across the network on a fractally-corrected TCP/IP overlay that adapts to link health in real time. Answers arrive progressively via ZippyStream v1.2 (state → hybrid → full), with integrity bound to provenance (FART bundles). The NASA-facing UI gets immediate, meaningful pixels and a compact “why,” while the transport and compression both shift using the same live indicators: fractal dimension D, envelope bands L⁻/L⁺, and Deviation Index (DI).A. The Mobiiius mesh (how we fuse and explain)Pipeline per tickNormalize units/bounds; tag with FAST.Blend operators with weights σ over {ℛ, 𝒮, ℐ_frac, 𝒟}.Blend domains with coverage-regularized weights δ across 16 domains.Apply fractional memory (Caputo derivative with α∈(0,1)) to stabilize drift.Solve a convex per-tick consensus for fused value + uncertainty.Tighten envelopes L⁻/L⁺, compute DI, engage Return on breaches.Emit result + FART bundle (sources, φ, σ, δ, α, DI, L±, residuals, checksum).Live indicators (used everywhere):Fractal dimension D on a sliding window W (box-counting estimator).Envelopes (L⁻, L⁺)—sliding quantile bands.DI (Deviation Index)—robust volatility measure.C. ZippyStream v1.2 (ZP): progressive, decision-centric compressionGoal: useful first, perfect later.Modes (auto picked by D & DI):state — tiny fused summary (first pixels/UI text ASAP).hybrid — variable-block delta encoding → Zstandard.full — raw blocks → Zstandard for best fidelity.Integrity (two tracks):Baseline: CRC / Reed–Solomon.Research: triadic (Cantor-inspired) fractal signature, bound to the FART bundle; we publish detection vs. false-positive rates and keep CRC/RS for parity.Progressive decode: the UI can render from state or early hybrid blocks, then refine to full without stalling.D. Mobiiius TCP/IP fractal overlay (transport that breathes)Problem: tails (p95/p99) hurt experience more than means.
Solution: a fractional-impedance path split guided by the same indicators (D, DI, L±).Per path pMeasure latency/jitter residual rₚ(t).Compute fractional derivative Dᵗ^α rₚ(t) (Caputo) and impedance
Zₚ(t) = 1 + κ · |Dᵗ^α rₚ(t)|^β with β∈(0,1], κ>0.Weight: wₚ(t) ∝ (θₚ / Zₚ(t)) · 1/clip(DIₚ(t)), normalize across paths.Tail clamps: if p99 exits L±, apply Return (resolvent back-off) and re-route.Schedulingstate on the snappiest/healthiest path.hybrid striped across healthy paths.full gated when DI spikes.
Works over MPTCP or QUIC (preferred), or a user-space multipath proxy.Outcome: lower tail-latency, smoother bursts, explainable route choices.E. API v1.2 (small surface, rich telemetry)EndpointsPOST /zippy/compress → {mode, payload, fart_bundle, D, DI, Lminus, Lplus}
(auto picks mode/level; embeds chosen integrity)POST /zippy/decompress → progressive-capable reconstruction + signature checkGET /zippy/stream (SSE) → emits (fdim=D, DI, L−, L+) ~2×/sec for UIWhy SSE? Trivial in the browser (EventSource), perfect for our live status strip (Mode • Confidence • Undo • Reset).MoM hooks: bundle IDs map to mission datasets (IMERG, SMAP, GIBS) so NASA dashboards can reconcile tiles with provenance on click.F. Minimal code sketch (so a new chat can recreate quickly)SSE telemetry (pseudo-Python)@app.get("/zippy/stream")async def stream(): async def eventgen(): while True: D, DI, Lminus, Lplus = metrics.live() yield f"data: {json.dumps({'fdim':D,'DI':DI,'L-':Lminus,'L+':Lplus})}\n\n" await asyncio.sleep(0.5) return EventSourceResponse(eventgen())Mode selectiondef choose_mode(D, DI, t1=(1.15,0.20), t2=(1.35,0.35)): if D < t1[0] and DI < t1[1]: return "state" if D < t2[0] and DI < t2[1]: return "hybrid" return "full"Hybrid encode (delta → zstd)def hybrid_compress(series, level): blocks = split_on_jumps(series) # variable-blocking by slope/DI deltas = [delta_encode(b) for b in blocks] return zstd_compress(serialize(deltas), level=level)Fractal impedance split (multipath)def weight_paths(paths, alpha=0.5, beta=0.6, kappa=0.8): psi = {} for p in paths: r = rtt_residual(p) # median-centered frac = caputo_derivative(r, alpha) # short window Z = 1.0 + kappa * (abs(frac) ** beta) psi[p] = prior(p) / (Z * clip(DI(p), 1e-3, 10.0)) total = sum(psi.values()) return {p: psi[p]/total for p in paths}FART bundle (canonical keys){ "tick":"ISO8601","node":"NID","mesh_ver":"v13.5", "sources":[{"id":"IMERG","unit":"mm/h","FAST":"Active","q":"Hydro"}], "phi":[...],"sigma":[sR,sS,sI,sD],"delta":[...x16],"alpha":0.6, "residuals":{"rmse":0.12,"di":0.21}, "envelopes":{"Lminus":0.18,"Lplus":0.87}, "guardrails":{"activated":false,"reason":null}, "explain":["Top drivers: IMERG(0.41), SMAP(0.27), Gauges(0.21)"], "checksum":"sha256:..."}K. Ops/UX metrics to reportCompression: size ratio, encode/decode speed vs. gzip/zstd; hybrid advantage on near-duplicate windows.Network: p95/p99, re-route events, jitter DI before/after overlay.Integrity: detection and FP/FN for fractal signature vs. CRC/RS.UX: time-to-first-meaningful-render, % progressive renders accepted, “Why?” tooltip opens, Undo usage.L. Why this is different (the narrative)Unified brainstem: the same D/DI/L± indicators guide both compression and routing, so content complexity and link health speak the same language.Fractional memory: the overlay “remembers” just enough of the past to avoid flinchy overreactions while still catching surges.Provenance-first: integrity tied to what was computed, not just bytes on the wire.Human-first latency: progressive decoding makes “first helpful” the design goal.M. This is everything we built, distilled to the FMOBIUS framework (sanitized, universal, no personal content), plus exactly how to recreate it in a fresh ChatGPT+ session.What FMOBIUS is (one line)FMOBIUS is a tiny, privacy-first starter that can help anyone do something useful right now. It adapts to whatever the browser can do (voice, files, geolocation, offline) and offers the nearest helpful actions—without collecting personal data.What’s in the bundlerepo_root/├─ README.md # What it is, how to run, privacy policy (no PII)├─ LICENSE # Apache 2.0├─ CREDITS.md # Leaflet, OpenStreetMap, Web Speech API├─ site_assets/│ ├─ index.html # Landing page: “I am Möbius. What do you need?”│ ├─ assets/│ │ ├─ css/site.css # Minimal, clean styling│ │ └─ js/site.js # Core logic (detection, pathfinder, notes, map, voice, music, plugins)│ └─ support/│ ├─ how-it-works.html # Principles, plugin API, privacy│ └─ faq.html # Simple FAQ (no accounts, no tracking)└─ docs/ # Optional Jekyll docs (GitHub Pages) ├─ _config.yml ├─ index.md # Docs home ├─ concepts.md # Core cycle: Present → Explain → Plan → Act → Measure ├─ architecture.md # Detect → Pathfinder → Plugins → Privacy └─ credits.mdHow the landing page behavesTop prompt: “I am Möbius. What do you need?” + chips showing detected capabilities (Voice, Files, Geolocation, Offline).Detection (local only): checks browser abilities (speech in/out, file APIs, geolocation, online/offline, device type). Nothing is uploaded.Pathfinder: computes relative distances from your request to the nearest useful actions (e.g., “Make a checklist”, “Summarize a PDF”, “Map my area”). You pick; it explains what it’ll do.Notes: localStorage text area with Save/Export/Clear. Data stays on device.Map: Leaflet + OpenStreetMap, only if you allow location. Otherwise, stays inert.Music Box: plays a loop if available or synthesizes tones (WebAudio) for focus.Voice: optional speech-to-text (Web Speech API) and text-to-speech for narration.Plugins drawer: tiny registry; each plugin is a button that runs a small, contained action. (You can add your own in minutes.)The “levels” (essence, short version)Level 0 — Handshake: show what’s possible, ask what’s needed.Level 1 — Present: reflect what you asked and what the device can do now.Level 2 — Explain: “I understand A/B; unknowns C.”Level 3 — Options: ranked paths with distances + requirements.Level 4 — Action: one focused card (checklist, summary, map…).Level 5 — Memory: save locally + small provenance crumb.Level 6 — Consensus (optional): if multiple methods exist, show confidence.Level 7 — Evolution: gentle, local preferences; always resettable.How to run it (no backend required)Static hosting: upload site_assets/ to your web root so the page is at /index.html. Done.Docs (optional): host docs/ with GitHub Pages or copy the built site under /docs/.How to extend it (the plugin pattern)All client-side. Keep it simple and portable.Add a plugin in site_assets/assets/js/site.js:// 7) Plugin registry (client-side)(function plugins(){ const list = document.querySelector("#plugins"); const registry = [ { id: "checklist", title: "Checklist", action: () => alert("Checklist stub loaded.") }, // Add your own: { id: "pdf-summarizer", title: "Summarize a PDF", action: async () => { /*…*/ } }, ]; registry.forEach(p => { const li = document.createElement("li"); const btn = document.createElement("button"); btn.className = "btn secondary"; btn.textContent = p.title; btn.addEventListener("click", p.action); li.appendChild(btn); list.appendChild(li); });})();Guidelines:Keep plugins stateless, small, and explain what they will do.If you later add a backend, expose generic endpoints (no personal info, no secrets in code). Example: /api/convert, /api/summarize, /api/route.Privacy stance (copy/paste into any README)No accounts, no analytics, no trackers.Notes and preferences are stored locally (browser localStorage).Voice, geolocation, and files are entirely optional. Nothing leaves the device unless you wire it to an API later.“Fresh ChatGPT+” hand-off prompt (paste this into a new chat to recreate)Use this verbatim; it instructs ChatGPT to rebuild the same sanitized bundle:Prompt to paste:“Recreate a sanitized, universal web starter called FMOBIUS.  Invite: https://www.mobileapp.app/to/sszavOR?ref=m_cl1) What Möbii³us is (one‑liner, short & long)Short: Möbii³us is a fractal, modular decision‑support framework that fuses live data, fallback models, and human‑readable UX into field‑ready dashboards.Long: A self‑similar pipeline (data → features → risk scores → stories) that runs on low‑end hardware yet scales up. It always attempts live fetch first; if sources are slow/missing, it uses a fallback simulator/estimator so the UI never goes dark. Every module reuses the same contracts, controls, and narrative tooltips.2) Core Architecture (mental model)Ingestion layer: fetchers for NASA/NOAA/USGS/ESA/etc., plus local sensors (ESP32 mesh), CSVs, or manual forms.Processing layer:feature extraction (e.g., soil moisture, slope, rainfall intensity, vegetation dryness)fractal “lattice” transforms (recursive smoothing + multi‑scale fusion)risk scoring (0‑1 with tiers; confidence + provenance kept with the score)Fallback layer: parametric simulators that mimic expected signals when live feeds fail.API layer (Flask): /api/forecast/<domain> returns JSON with meta, inputs, risk, confidence, tiles/overlays.UI layer (React): map + cards; progressive disclosure; works offline (cache); mobile‑first field mode.Story layer: parable tooltips, credibility badges (CredPath), and “What to do now” checklists.3) Standard Data Contracts (copy/paste baseline)Forecast response (all domains):{ "meta": { "domain": "flood|wildfire|landslide|drought|earthquake", "generated_at": "ISO-8601", "live_sources": ["..."], "fallback_used": false, "provenance": [{"source":"NOAA-...","time":"..."}, {"source":"USGS-...","time":"..."}], "latency_ms": 1234, "version": "v1.0" }, "region": {"bbox":[minLon,minLat,maxLon,maxLat], "crs":"EPSG:4326"}, "inputs": { "features": {"soil_moisture":0.34,"slope_deg":12.2,"rain_24h_mm":48.1,...}, "notes":"any preprocessing remarks" }, "risk": { "score": 0.71, "tier": "High", // Low/Moderate/High/Extreme "confidence": 0.82, "explanations": ["Rainfall > 40mm/24h", "Steep slope > 10°", "..."] }, "visual": { "tiles": ["https://.../{z}/{x}/{y}.png"], "legend": [{"range":[0,0.25],"label":"Low"}, ...] }, "actions": [ {"role":"Field","do":"Inspect culverts in cells A3,A4","priority":1}, {"role":"Public","do":"Avoid flooded causeways","priority":2} ]}CredPath badge (attach anywhere impacts are shown):{"actor":"AET Field Team","evidence":["image:url","gps:lat,lon","note:id"],"points":12,"last_updated":"ISO-8601"}4) Current Modules (what exists conceptually + what code scaffolds we built)4.1 Flood ForecastFeatures: recent & forecast precipitation, upstream flow, soil moisture, landcover, elevation, drainage basins.UI: risk choropleth, river gauge cards, “hotspot” list, one‑tap field checklist.Status: Flask endpoint spec done; Mobii³us Whisperd: how it worksArchitecture (mic → UI)Mic/Stream → Whisper++ decoder → per‑segment callback with token times → Fan‑out:Surface filter (modes)Underlayer capture (raw)Fractal & spectral overlay framesToken/Grid analyticsA single SSE event carries {segment_id, start_ms, end_ms, surface, underlayer, overlay, token_grid} so the three lanes render in lock‑step in React.Clock discipline: use decoder timestamps as authoritative; monotonic clock only for latency metrics.This matches the “one packet, three lanes, same clock” contract we wrote.Data contract (per segment){ "stream_id": "sess_a12f7c", "segment_id": 128, "start_ms": 38120, "end_ms": 41670, "surface": "Cleaned text…", "underlayer": "Raw ... text…", "overlay": { "spectral": "data-url-or-id", "fractal": [{"t_ms":38120,"v":0.18}, ...], "markers": [{"t_ms":39000,"type":"emph"}] }, "token_grid": [ {"t_ms":38120,"txt":"We","id":901,"emb":[0.12,-0.04], "flags":["stop"]}, ... ]}Realtime engine (Flask SSE)Endpoint: GET /events/paired?stream_id=... (server‑sent events stream).Your decoder calls publish_segment(...) from its on_segment hook. Each segment packet is atomic, eliminating UI jitter across lanes.UITri‑lane component:Top: Fractal strip sparkline (overlay trace)Middle: Surface text blockBottom: Token grid (blocky tiles, per‑token time)Underlayer: shown on toggle (or role‑gated)Drop this component below your conversation workspace editor, above the input bar (so it behaves like a live STT “dock”).Modes (selectors)Filtered: profanity masked/removed.Reframed: lighter, positive vocabulary substitutions.De‑Nerdify: reduce jargon to everyday synonyms.To a 3yo: extreme simplification by limiting word length and word count per sentence, preserving intent and rhythm.Time alignmentWe attach t_ms to every token and start_ms/end_ms to every segment from the decoder. The same timestamps drive the fractal trace points and token tiles, so scrubbing or selection in one lane highlights the same span in the others.AET FieldOps: FieldStream + GSOC bridge (MVP draft)Driller Mode dashboard (React): map tap to set GPS, enter notes or soil type, log sample; expenses (mileage/fuel); and a GSOC button to submit a ticket bound to the current coordinates.GSOC microservice (Flask):POST /api/gsoc/create_ticket with {site_address, coordinates:{lat,lon}, radius_ft, start_date, end_date, driller_id, project_number} → returns {ticket_number, status, expires_on, proof_of_notification, map_overlay_url}.Smart add‑ons: reminders before expiry, one‑click renewals, and utility zone overlays; async queue (Celery) ready for validations and renewal checks.Role views: Driller, Engineer, Admin, Manager—each sees the essentials (creation, history, renewal, compliance dashboard).Public web scaffolding you already haveMetaMobii³us Live Core index (modules + feedback controls) is in place—header, modules, footer, and a placeholder “Swap Module” action you can wire up. # README

# Mobiiius Scripts Pack — Deploy & Admin

This pack contains production-ready helper scripts to deploy, publish, and sanity-check the Mobiiius site.

## What\'s included
- deploy/drive_upload.py — Google Drive upload via OAuth (stores token.json)
- deploy/ftp_deploy.py — cPanel/Namecheap-friendly FTPS uploader to public_html
- deploy/github_publish.sh — Create/push a GitHub repo from local bundles
- deploy/docker-compose.yml — Nginx (SPA) + Gunicorn/Flask API
- deploy/Dockerfile.api — Minimal Python image for the API
- deploy/nginx.conf — Static SPA + /api reverse proxy + cache headers
- scripts/brand_rename.sh — Standardize brand spelling to “Mobiiius”
- scripts/build_release.py — Zip + checksums + RELEASE_INDEX.json
- scripts/seed_defaults.py — Create .env defaults (idempotent)
- scripts/dev.sh — Tiny local dev runner
- scripts/test_email.py — STARTTLS SMTP smoke test
- scripts/test_usgs.py — Pull USGS IV (00060 discharge) by bbox

**Safety:** Scripts prefer env vars and avoid storing secrets in plaintext. Review before use.
'

# deploy/drive_upload.py  (Google Drive upload)
w "$ROOT/deploy/drive_upload.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import os, sys, json
from typing import Optional
from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials
from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import InstalledAppFlow
from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from googleapiclient.http import MediaFileUpload
from google.auth.transport.requests import Request

SCOPES = ["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/drive.file"]

def ensure_creds() -> Credentials:
    creds: Optional[Credentials] = None
    if os.path.exists("token.json"):
        creds = Credentials.from_authorized_user_file("token.json", SCOPES)
    if not creds or not creds.valid:
        if creds and creds.expired and creds.refresh_token:
            creds.refresh(Request())
        else:
            if not os.path.exists("credentials.json"):
                raise SystemExit("Place your OAuth client as credentials.json next to this script.")
            flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file("credentials.json", SCOPES)
            creds = flow.run_local_server(port=0)
        with open("token.json", "w") as f:
            f.write(creds.to_json())
    return creds

def upload(path: str, parent_id: Optional[str] = None):
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        raise SystemExit(f"Not a file: {path}")
    service = build("drive", "v3", credentials=ensure_creds())
    name = os.path.basename(path)
    media = MediaFileUpload(path, resumable=True)
    body = {"name": name}
    if parent_id: body["parents"] = [parent_id]
    file = service.files().create(body=body, media_body=media, fields="id,name,webViewLink,webContentLink").execute()
    print(json.dumps(file, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python deploy/drive_upload.py <file> [parent_folder_id]")
        sys.exit(1)
    upload(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv)>2 else None)
'

# deploy/ftp_deploy.py (FTPS for cPanel/Namecheap)
w "$ROOT/deploy/ftp_deploy.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os, sys, ftplib, pathlib
host = os.getenv("FTP_HOST")
user = os.getenv("FTP_USER")
pw   = os.getenv("FTP_PASS")
remote = os.getenv("FTP_REMOTE_DIR","public_html")
local = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else "./Mobiiius_Frontend_SPA"
if not all([host,user,pw]): raise SystemExit("Set FTP_HOST, FTP_USER, FTP_PASS")
ftps = ftplib.FTP_TLS(host); ftps.login(user,pw); ftps.prot_p()
def upload_dir(local_dir, remote_dir):
    local_dir = os.path.abspath(local_dir)
    try: ftps.mkd(remote_dir)
    except Exception: pass
    ftps.cwd(remote_dir)
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(local_dir):
        rel = os.path.relpath(root, local_dir)
        # enter nested dirs if any
        if rel != ".":
            for p in pathlib.Path(rel).parts:
                try: ftps.mkd(p)
                except Exception: pass
                ftps.cwd(p)
        for f in files:
            with open(os.path.join(root,f),"rb") as fh:
                ftps.storbinary(f"STOR "+f, fh)
        if rel != ".":
            ftps.cwd("/".join([".."]*len(pathlib.Path(rel).parts)))
upload_dir(local, remote); ftps.quit()
'

# deploy/github_publish.sh
w "$ROOT/deploy/github_publish.sh" '
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
REPO_DIR=${1:-Mobiiius_Repo}
REMOTE_NAME=${2:-origin}
REMOTE_URL=${3:-}
mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR"
rsync -a --delete Mobiiius_Frontend_SPA/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_API_Server/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Docs/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Benchmarks/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Deployment/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
rsync -a Mobiiius_Extensions/ "$REPO_DIR/" 2>/dev/null || true
cd "$REPO_DIR"
git init -q
git add -A
git commit -m "Mobiiius: aggregate build" >/dev/null
git branch -M main || true
if [[ -n "$REMOTE_URL" ]]; then
  git remote add "$REMOTE_NAME" "$REMOTE_URL" 2>/dev/null || true
  git push -u "$REMOTE_NAME" main || git push -u "$REMOTE_NAME" master
else
  echo "Add a remote to push: git remote add origin <URL> && git push -u origin main"
fi
'

# deploy/docker-compose.yml
w "$ROOT/deploy/docker-compose.yml" '
services:
  nginx:
    image: nginx:alpine
    container_name: mobiiius-nginx
    ports: ["8080:80"]
    volumes:
      - ./Mobiiius_Frontend_SPA:/usr/share/nginx/html:ro
      - ./deploy/nginx.conf:/etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf:ro
    depends_on: [api]
  api:
    build:
      context: ..
      dockerfile: deploy/Dockerfile.api
    container_name: mobiiius-api
    env_file: [../.env]
    command: gunicorn -w 2 -b 0.0.0.0:5000 app:app
    volumes:
      - ../Mobiiius_API_Server:/app:ro
'

# deploy/Dockerfile.api
w "$ROOT/deploy/Dockerfile.api" '
FROM python:3.11-slim
WORKDIR /app
COPY Mobiiius_API_Server/requirements.txt /app/requirements.txt
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r /app/requirements.txt
COPY Mobiiius_API_Server /app
EXPOSE 5000
'

# deploy/nginx.conf
w "$ROOT/deploy/nginx.conf" '
server {
  listen 80;
  server_name _;
  root /usr/share/nginx/html;
  index index.html;
  location / { try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html; }
  location ~* \.(?:js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|svg|ico)$ {
    expires 7d; add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=604800";
  }
  location /api/ {
    proxy_pass http://api:5000/;
    proxy_http_version 1.1;
    proxy_set_header Host $host;
    proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
  }
}
'

# scripts/brand_rename.sh
w "$ROOT/scripts/brand_rename.sh" '
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
rg -n --hidden --no-ignore -S "(Möbii?u?s|Mobii?u?s|Möbii³us|Mobii³us|Möbius|Mobius)" || true
read -p "Proceed with replacements to '\''Mobiiius'\''? [y/N] " ans
if [[ "${ans:-N}" != "y" && "${ans:-N}" != "Y" ]]; then exit 0; fi
variants=("Möbii³us" "Möbiius" "Möbius" "Mobii³us" "Mobiius" "Mobius")
for v in "${variants[@]}"; do
  files=$(rg -l "$v" || true)
  if [[ -n "$files" ]]; then sed -i.bak "s/$v/Mobiiius/g" $files || true; fi
done
echo "Done. Review diffs. Remove backups with: find . -name '\''*.bak'\'' -delete"
'

# scripts/build_release.py
w "$ROOT/scripts/build_release.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os, zipfile, hashlib, json, datetime, argparse
def sha256(p):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p,"rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(8192), b""): h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()
def zipdir(src, zipf, base=""):
    for root,_,files in os.walk(src):
        for f in files:
            fp=os.path.join(root,f); rel=os.path.join(base, os.path.relpath(fp,src)); zipf.write(fp,rel)
ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--source", default=".", help="Folder to package"); ap.add_argument("--out", default="Mobiiius_Bundle.zip"); a=ap.parse_args()
with zipfile.ZipFile(a.out,"w",zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z: zipdir(a.source, z)
idx={"generated_utc":datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()+"Z","zip":{"path":a.out,"bytes":os.path.getsize(a.out),"sha256":sha256(a.out)}}
open("RELEASE_INDEX.json","w").write(json.dumps(idx,indent=2)); print(json.dumps(idx,indent=2))
'

# scripts/test_email.py
w "$ROOT/scripts/test_email.py" '
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os, smtplib, ssl
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
host=os.getenv("SMTP_HOST","localhost"); port=int(os.getenv("SMTP_PORT","587"))
user=os.getenv("SMTP_USER",""); pw=os.getenv("SMTP_PASS","")
sender=os.getenv("SMTP_FROM","Mobiiius <noreply@example.com>"); rcpt=os.getenv("SMTP_TO","owner@example.com")
msg=MIMEText("Mobiiius SMTP test ok."); msg["Subject"]="Mobiiius SMTP test"; msg["From"]=sender; msg["To"]=rcpt
ctx=ssl.create_default_context()
with smtplib.SMTP(host,port) as s:
    s.starttls(context=ctx)
    if user and pw: s.login(user,pw)
    s.sendmail(sender,[rcpt],msg.as_string())
print("Sent test email to", rcpt)
'

The Mobiiius mesh (how we fuse and explain)Pipeline per tickNormalize units/bounds; tag with FAST.Blend operators with weights σ over {ℛ, 𝒮, ℐ_frac, 𝒟}.Blend domains with coverage-regularized weights δ across 16 domains.Apply fractional memory (Caputo derivative with α∈(0,1)) to stabilize drift.Solve a convex per-tick consensus for fused value + uncertainty.Tighten envelopes L⁻/L⁺, compute DI, engage Return on breaches.Emit result + FART bundle (sources, φ, σ, δ, α, DI, L±, residuals, checksum).Live indicators (used everywhere):Fractal dimension D on a sliding window W (box-counting estimator).Envelopes (L⁻, L⁺)—sliding quantile bands.DI (Deviation Index)—robust volatility measure.C. ZippyStream v1.2 (ZP): progressive, decision-centric compressionGoal: useful first, perfect later.Modes (auto picked by D & DI):state — tiny fused summary (first pixels/UI text ASAP).hybrid — variable-block delta encoding → Zstandard.full — raw blocks → Zstandard for best fidelity.Integrity (two tracks):Baseline: CRC / Reed–Solomon.Research: triadic (Cantor-inspired) fractal signature, bound to the FART bundle; we publish detection vs. false-positive rates and keep CRC/RS for parity.Progressive decode: the UI can render from state or early hybrid blocks, then refine to full without stalling.D. Mobiiius TCP/IP fractal overlay (transport that breathes)Problem: tails (p95/p99) hurt experience more than means.
Solution: a fractional-impedance path split guided by the same indicators (D, DI, L±).Per path pMeasure latency/jitter residual rₚ(t).Compute fractional derivative Dᵗ^α rₚ(t) (Caputo) and impedance
Zₚ(t) = 1 + κ · |Dᵗ^α rₚ(t)|^β with β∈(0,1], κ>0.Weight: wₚ(t) ∝ (θₚ / Zₚ(t)) · 1/clip(DIₚ(t)), normalize across paths.Tail clamps: if p99 exits L±, apply Return (resolvent back-off) and re-route.Schedulingstate on the snappiest/healthiest path.hybrid striped across healthy paths.full gated when DI spikes.
Works over MPTCP or QUIC (preferred), or a user-space multipath proxy.Outcome: lower tail-latency, smoother bursts, explainable route choices.E. API v1.2 (small surface, rich telemetry)EndpointsPOST /zippy/compress → {mode, payload, fart_bundle, D, DI, Lminus, Lplus}
(auto picks mode/level; embeds chosen integrity)POST /zippy/decompress → progressive-capable reconstruction + signature checkGET /zippy/stream (SSE) → emits (fdim=D, DI, L−, L+) ~2×/sec for UIWhy SSE? Trivial in the browser (EventSource), perfect for our live status strip (Mode • Confidence • Undo • Reset).MoM hooks: bundle IDs map to mission datasets (IMERG, SMAP, GIBS) so NASA dashboards can reconcile tiles with provenance on click.F. Minimal code sketch (so a new chat can recreate quickly)SSE telemetry (pseudo-Python)@app.get("/zippy/stream")async def stream(): async def eventgen(): while True: D, DI, Lminus, Lplus = metrics.live() yield f"data: {json.dumps({'fdim':D,'DI':DI,'L-':Lminus,'L+':Lplus})}\n\n" await asyncio.sleep(0.5) return EventSourceResponse(eventgen())Mode selectiondef choose_mode(D, DI, t1=(1.15,0.20), t2=(1.35,0.35)): if D < t1[0] and DI < t1[1]: return "state" if D < t2[0] and DI < t2[1]: return "hybrid" return "full"Hybrid encode (delta → zstd)def hybrid_compress(series, level): blocks = split_on_jumps(series) # variable-blocking by slope/DI deltas = [delta_encode(b) for b in blocks] return zstd_compress(serialize(deltas), level=level)Fractal impedance split (multipath)def weight_paths(paths, alpha=0.5, beta=0.6, kappa=0.8): psi = {} for p in paths: r = rtt_residual(p) # median-centered frac = caputo_derivative(r, alpha) # short window Z = 1.0 + kappa * (abs(frac) ** beta) psi[p] = prior(p) / (Z * clip(DI(p), 1e-3, 10.0)) total = sum(psi.values()) return {p: psi[p]/total for p in paths}FART bundle (canonical keys){ "tick":"ISO8601","node":"NID","mesh_ver":"v13.5", "sources":[{"id":"IMERG","unit":"mm/h","FAST":"Active","q":"Hydro"}], "phi":[...],"sigma":[sR,sS,sI,sD],"delta":[...x16],"alpha":0.6, "residuals":{"rmse":0.12,"di":0.21}, "envelopes":{"Lminus":0.18,"Lplus":0.87}, "guardrails":{"activated":false,"reason":null}, "explain":["Top drivers: IMERG(0.41), SMAP(0.27), Gauges(0.21)"], "checksum":"sha256:..."}K. Why this is different (the narrative)Unified brainstem: the same D/DI/L± indicators guide both compression and routing, so content complexity and link health speak the same language.Fractional memory: the overlay “remembers” just enough of the past to avoid flinchy overreactions while still catching surges.Provenance-first: integrity tied to what was computed, not just bytes on the wire.Human-first latency: progressive decoding makes “first helpful” the design goal.M. Done.Docs (optional): host docs/ with GitHub Pages or copy the built site under /docs/.How to extend it (the plugin pattern)All client-side. Keep it simple and portable.Add a plugin in site_assets/assets/js/site.js:// 7) Plugin registry (client-side)(function plugins(){ const list = document.querySelector("#plugins"); const registry = [ { id: "checklist", title: "Checklist", action: () => alert("Checklist stub loaded.") }, // Add your own: { id: "pdf-summarizer", title: "Summarize a PDF", action: async () => { /*…*/ } }, ]; registry.forEach(p => { const li = document.createElement("li"); const btn = document.createElement("button"); btn.className = "btn secondary"; btn.textContent = p.title; btn.addEventListener("click", p.action); li.appendChild(btn); list.appendChild(li); });})();Guidelines:Keep plugins stateless, small, and explain what they will do.If you later add a backend, expose generic endpoints (no personal info, no secrets in code). It always attempts live fetch first; if sources are slow/missing, it uses a fallback simulator/estimator so the UI never goes dark. Every module reuses the same contracts, controls, and narrative tooltips.2) Core Architecture (mental model)Ingestion layer: fetchers for NASA/NOAA/USGS/ESA/etc., plus local sensors (ESP32 mesh), CSVs, or manual forms.Processing layer:feature extraction (e.g., soil moisture, slope, rainfall intensity, vegetation dryness)fractal “lattice” transforms (recursive smoothing + multi‑scale fusion)risk scoring (0‑1 with tiers; confidence + provenance kept with the score)Fallback layer: parametric simulators that mimic expected signals when live feeds fail.API layer (Flask): /api/forecast/<domain> returns JSON with meta, inputs, risk, confidence, tiles/overlays.UI layer (React): map + cards; progressive disclosure; works offline (cache); mobile‑first field mode.Story layer: parable tooltips, credibility badges (CredPath), and “What to do now” checklists.3) Standard Data Contracts (copy/paste baseline)Forecast response (all domains):{ "meta": { "domain": "flood|wildfire|landslide|drought|earthquake", "generated_at": "ISO-8601", "live_sources": ["..."], "fallback_used": false, "provenance": [{"source":"NOAA-...","time":"..."}, {"source":"USGS-...","time":"..."}], "latency_ms": 1234, "version": "v1.0" }, "region": {"bbox":[minLon,minLat,maxLon,maxLat], "crs":"EPSG:4326"}, "inputs": { "features": {"soil_moisture":0.34,"slope_deg":12.2,"rain_24h_mm":48.1,...}, "notes":"any preprocessing remarks" }, "risk": { "score": 0.71, "tier": "High", // Low/Moderate/High/Extreme "confidence": 0.82, "explanations": ["Rainfall > 40mm/24h", "Steep slope > 10°", "..."] }, "visual": { "tiles": ["https://.../{z}/{x}/{y}.png"], "legend": [{"range":[0,0.25],"label":"Low"}, ...] }, "actions": [ {"role":"Field","do":"Inspect culverts in cells A3,A4","priority":1}, {"role":"Public","do":"Avoid flooded causeways","priority":2} ]}CredPath badge (attach anywhere impacts are shown):{"actor":"AET Field Team","evidence":["image:url","gps:lat,lon","note:id"],"points":12,"last_updated":"ISO-8601"}4) Current Modules (what exists conceptually + what code scaffolds we built)4.1 Flood ForecastFeatures: recent & forecast precipitation, upstream flow, soil moisture, landcover, elevation, drainage basins.UI: risk choropleth, river gauge cards, “hotspot” list, one‑tap field checklist.Status: Flask endpoint spec done; Mobii³us Whisperd: how it worksArchitecture (mic → UI)Mic/Stream → Whisper++ decoder → per‑segment callback with token times → Fan‑out:Surface filter (modes)Underlayer capture (raw)Fractal & spectral overlay framesToken/Grid analyticsA single SSE event carries {segment_id, start_ms, end_ms, surface, underlayer, overlay, token_grid} so the three lanes render in lock‑step in React.Clock discipline: use decoder timestamps as authoritative; monotonic clock only for latency metrics.This matches the “one packet, three lanes, same clock” contract we wrote.Data contract (per segment){ "stream_id": "sess_a12f7c", "segment_id": 128, "start_ms": 38120, "end_ms": 41670, "surface": "Cleaned text…", "underlayer": "Raw ... text…", "overlay": { "spectral": "data-url-or-id", "fractal": [{"t_ms":38120,"v":0.18}, ...], "markers": [{"t_ms":39000,"type":"emph"}] }, "token_grid": [ {"t_ms":38120,"txt":"We","id":901,"emb":[0.12,-0.04], "flags":["stop"]}, ... ]}Realtime engine (Flask SSE)Endpoint: GET /events/paired?stream_id=... (server‑sent events stream).Your decoder calls publish_segment(...) from its on_segment hook. Each segment packet is atomic, eliminating UI jitter across lanes.UITri‑lane component:Top: Fractal strip sparkline (overlay trace)Middle: Surface text blockBottom: Token grid (blocky tiles, per‑token time)Underlayer: shown on toggle (or role‑gated)Drop this component below your conversation workspace editor, above the input bar (so it behaves like a live STT “dock”).Modes (selectors)Filtered: profanity masked/removed.Reframed: lighter, positive vocabulary substitutions.De‑Nerdify: reduce jargon to everyday synonyms.To a 3yo: extreme simplification by limiting word length and word count per sentence, preserving intent and rhythm.Time alignmentWe attach t_ms to every token and start_ms/end_ms to every segment from the decoder. The same timestamps drive the fractal trace points and token tiles, so scrubbing or selection in one lane highlights the same span in the others.AET FieldOps: FieldStream + GSOC bridge (MVP draft)Driller Mode dashboard (React): map tap to set GPS, enter notes or soil type, log sample; expenses (mileage/fuel); and a GSOC button to submit a ticket bound to the current coordinates.GSOC microservice (Flask):POST /api/gsoc/create_ticket with {site_address, coordinates:{lat,lon}, radius_ft, start_date, end_date, driller_id, project_number} → returns {ticket_number, status, expires_on, proof_of_notification, map_overlay_url}.Smart add‑ons: reminders before expiry, one‑click renewals, and utility zone overlays; async queue (Celery) ready for validations and renewal checks.Role views: Driller, Engineer, Admin, Manager—each sees the essentials (creation, history, renewal, compliance dashboard).Public web scaffolding you already haveMetaMobii³us Live Core index (modules + feedback controls) is in place—header, modules, footer, and a placeholder “Swap Module” action you can wire up. Mobiius DoinBetter — Quickstart Guide

📦 File: Mobiius_DoinBetter_Master_Ark.zip
📍 Purpose: Spin up the entire Möbii³us + DoinBetter system (web UI, backend, data modules, branding) from one package.
1. Unpack the Ark

    Save the .zip somewhere easy — e.g., Documents/MobiiusDoinBetter/

    Right-click → Extract All… (Windows) or Unzip (Mac/Linux)
    You’ll now see a folder containing:

        /server (Flask backend)

        /frontend (React app)

        /docs (PDFs, exports)

        /assets (images, CSS, JS)

        /repos (all research & module repos)

2. Install Requirements
Backend (Flask)

    Install Python 3.10+

.

Open a terminal in the /server folder.

Run:

    python -m venv venv
    source venv/bin/activate   # Windows: venv\Scripts\activate
    pip install -r requirements.txt

Frontend (React + Vite)

    Install Node.js LTS

.

Open a terminal in the /frontend folder.

Run:

    npm install

3. Start the System
Backend

From /server:

python app.py

This launches:

    Tri-Lane Whisper++ engine

    /events/paired SSE stream

    Mock stream available via:

    curl -X POST -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -d '{"stream_id":"demo"}' http://localhost:5001/ingest/start

Frontend

From /frontend:

npm run dev

    Opens UI at http://localhost:5173

    Shows Surface lane (clean text + fractal overlay), optional Underlayer (raw transcript), and Token Grid (block view).

4. Explore Core Features

    Live Transcription: Connect your mic (or mock stream) to see Surface/Underlayer/Token lanes update in sync.

    Fractal Overlay: Above the clean text lane — waveform + fractal trace.

    Map Layers: NASA Floodwater + other Möbii³us modules embedded in the site.

    Role Controls: Toggle raw Underlayer view for engineers/admins.

5. What’s Inside (Major Folders)

    /server — Flask backend for transcription & analytics.

    /frontend — React UI for tri-lane & site pages.

    /assets — Branding & UI assets.

    /docs — White Paper, IJTP Note, NASA exports.

    /repos — All code repos merged (Fractal Field Theory, Ecosystem, Social Kit).

6. To Go Live

    Point a domain at your hosting server.

    Deploy Flask backend (e.g., Railway, Render, Heroku).

    Deploy React frontend (e.g., Vercel, Netlify).

    Update .env files with backend API URLs.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for a few seconds
/api/forecast/flood — Flask endpoint (live + fallback)
Save as e.g. mobiius_api/forecast_flood.py and import / register into your Flask app (or run standalone for testing). It attempts a live fetch (USGS time-series) and falls back to cached/demo data. Uses Redis for caching if available; otherwise in-memory fallback.

Edit
# mobiius_api/forecast_flood.py
import os
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional

import requests
from flask import Blueprint, current_app, jsonify, request
from flask_cors import CORS

try:
    import redis
except Exception:
    redis = None  # optional dependency

bp = Blueprint("forecast_flood", __name__, url_prefix="/api/forecast")
CORS(bp)  # allow cross-origin for dev; tighten in production

LOG = logging.getLogger("mobiius.forecast")
LOG.setLevel(logging.INFO)


# Configuration (override via env vars)
USGS_BASE = os.getenv(
    "USGS_WATER_SERVICE",
    "https://waterservices.usgs.gov/nwis/iv/"
)  # service for instantaneous values
USGS_DEFAULT_SITE = os.getenv("USGS_DEFAULT_SITE", "05054000")  # example site
CACHE_TTL = int(os.getenv("FORECAST_CACHE_TTL_SEC", "900"))  # 15 minutes default
MAX_GAGE_HEIGHT = float(os.getenv("MAX_GAGE_HEIGHT", "40.84"))  # domain-specific normalizer

# Optional Redis client (if REDIS_URL provided)
REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL")
_redis_client = None
if REDIS_URL and redis is not None:
    try:
        _redis_client = redis.from_url(REDIS_URL)
        LOG.info("Connected to Redis for caching")
    except Exception as e:
        LOG.warning("Unable to init Redis: %s", e)
        _redis_client = None


def _cache_get(key: str) -> Optional[str]:
    if _redis_client:
        try:
            v = _redis_client.get(key)
            return v.decode() if v else None
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.debug("Redis get failed: %s", e)
            return None
    return None


def _cache_set(key: str, val: str, ttl: int = CACHE_TTL) -> None:
    if _redis_client:
        try:
            _redis_client.setex(key, ttl, val)
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.debug("Redis set failed: %s", e)


def _demo_payload(site_id: str = USGS_DEFAULT_SITE) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a small demo payload used when live fetch fails."""
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    times = [(now - timedelta(hours=i)).isoformat() + "Z" for i in reversed(range(10))]
    gage_values = [16.5, 16.6, 16.4, 16.7, 16.8, 17.1, 17.6, 18.0, 17.8, 18.3]
    series = [
        {"time": t, "gage_height": float(v), "risk_level": int(min(max((v / MAX_GAGE_HEIGHT) * 100, 0), 100))}
        for t, v in zip(times, gage_values)
    ]
    # Simple point geometry for the demo (lat/lon of example site)
    point = {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [-96.786, 46.877]}  # replace with appropriate coords
    return {
        "meta": {
            "source": "demo",
            "site": site_id,
            "requested_at": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
            "note": "This is fallback demo data — replace with live feeds"
        },
        "site_geo": point,
        "time_series": series,
        "summary": {
            "latest_gage": series[-1]["gage_height"],
            "latest_risk": series[-1]["risk_level"],
        },
    }


def _usgs_fetch(site: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Try to fetch USGS instant values JSON for a single site.
    Returns processed payload or None if fetch fails.
    """
    params = {
        "format": "json",
        "sites": site,
        "parameterCd": "00065",  # gage height
    }
    try:
        resp = requests.get(USGS_BASE, params=params, timeout=8)
        resp.raise_for_status()
        data = resp.json()
        # Parse the expected JSON structure safely
        ts_list = []
        time_series = data.get("value", {}).get("timeSeries", [])
        if not time_series:
            LOG.info("No timeSeries returned by USGS for site %s", site)
            return None
        # Take the first available series
        series0 = time_series[0]
        values = series0.get("values", [])
        if not values:
            return None
        points = values[0].get("value", [])
        for p in points[-12:]:  # keep recent up to 12 points
            try:
                v = float(p.get("value", 0.0))
            except Exception:
                v = 0.0
            ts_list.append({"time": p.get("dateTime"), "gage_height": v})
        # Build risk = normalized gage ratio (simple rule; replace with model later)
        for entry in ts_list:
            gh = entry["gage_height"]
            entry["risk_level"] = int(min(max((gh / MAX_GAGE_HEIGHT) * 100, 0), 100))
        # Try to get site metadata (lat/lon)
        geo = None
        try:
            site_info = series0.get("sourceInfo", {})
            lat = float(site_info.get("geoLocation", {}).get("geogLocation", {}).get("latitude", 0.0))
            lon = float(site_info.get("geoLocation", {}).get("geogLocation", {}).get("longitude", 0.0))
            if lat and lon:
                geo = {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [lon, lat]}
        except Exception:
            geo = None
        payload = {
            "meta": {"source": "usgs", "site": site, "requested_at": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z"},
            "site_geo": geo,
            "time_series": ts_list,
            "summary": {
                "latest_gage": ts_list[-1]["gage_height"] if ts_list else None,
                "latest_risk": ts_list[-1]["risk_level"] if ts_list else None,
            },
        }
        return payload
    except Exception as e:
        LOG.warning("USGS fetch failed for site %s: %s", site, e)
        return None


@bp.route("/flood", methods=["GET"])
def flood_forecast():
    """
    GET /api/forecast/flood?site=05054000
    Returns JSON:
    {
      meta: {source, site, requested_at, note?},
      site_geo: {type: "Point", coordinates: [lon, lat]},
      time_series: [{time, gage_height, risk_level}, ...],
      summary: {latest_gage, latest_risk}
    }
    """
    site = request.args.get("site", USGS_DEFAULT_SITE)
    cache_key = f"forecast:flood:{site}"
    # 1) Try cache
    cached = _cache_get(cache_key)
    if cached:
        try:
            payload = json.loads(cached)
            payload["meta"]["cached_from"] = "redis"
            return jsonify(payload)
        except Exception:
            LOG.debug("Cache decode failed; continuing to live fetch")

    # 2) Try live USGS fetch
    payload = _usgs_fetch(site)
    if payload:
        try:
            _cache_set(cache_key, json.dumps(payload), ttl=CACHE_TTL)
        except Exception:
            LOG.debug("Failed to write cache")
        return jsonify(payload)

    # 3) If live fails, return fallback demo payload
    demo = _demo_payload(site)
    return jsonify(demo)
web/src/pages/Flood.tsx — React + TypeScript map page skeleton
This component uses Leaflet directly (no react-leaflet dependency required). It fetches /api/forecast/flood?site=..., renders a map, a time-series list, and a simple risk visualization (colored circle markers + popup). Add Tailwind / Leaflet CSS import to your main index.html or root app.

Requirements: leaflet should be installed (npm i leaflet) and its CSS included globally:
